import { Component, OnInit, OnDestroy, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { Subscription } from 'rxjs';
import { InventoryService, Inventory } from '../../../../services/inventory.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-inventory-search',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule],
  templateUrl: './inventory-search.html',
  styleUrl: './inventory-search.css'
})
export class InventorySearchComponent implements OnInit, OnDestroy {
  private inventoryService = inject(InventoryService);
  private inventorySub!: Subscription;

  inventoryList: Inventory[] = [];
  filteredInventory: Inventory[] = [];
  searchValue: string = '';
  isLoading = false;

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.inventoryService.getInventories();
    
    this.inventorySub = this.inventoryService.getInventoryListUpdateListener()
      .subscribe((inventories: Inventory[]) => {
        this.isLoading = false;
        this.inventoryList = inventories;
        this.filteredInventory = inventories;
      });
  }

  onSearch(): void {
    if (!this.searchValue.trim()) {
      this.filteredInventory = this.inventoryList;
      return;
    }
    const term = this.searchValue.toLowerCase();
    this.filteredInventory = this.inventoryList.filter(item =>
      item.name.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
      item.batchId.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
      item.email.toLowerCase().includes(term)
    );
  }

  onDelete(id: string): void {
    if (confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this drug item?')) {
      this.inventoryService.deleteInventory(id);
    }
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    if (this.inventorySub) {
      this.inventorySub.unsubscribe();
    }
  }
}

import { Component, OnInit, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormBuilder, FormGroup, ReactiveFormsModule, Validators } from '@angular/forms';
import { InventoryService } from '../../services/inventory.service';
import { Inventory as InventoryModel } from '../../services/inventory.model';
import { ApiService } from '../../services/api.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-inventory',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, ReactiveFormsModule],
  templateUrl: './inventory.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./inventory.component.css']
})
export class Inventory implements OnInit {
  inventoryItems: InventoryModel[] = [];
  filteredItems: InventoryModel[] = [];
  readonly inventoryItemsSignal = signal<InventoryModel[]>([]);
  searchText = '';
  selectedItem: InventoryModel | null = null;
  loading = false;
  inventoryForm!: FormGroup;
  errorMessage = '';
  successMessage = '';
  isEditing = false;

  currentPage = 1;
  pageSize = 10;
  totalItems = 0;
  totalPages = 1;

  sortColumn = '';
  sortAscending = true;
  inventorySub: import("rxjs").Subscription | undefined;

  constructor(private inventoryService: InventoryService, private fb: FormBuilder, private api: ApiService) {}

ngOnInit(): void {
    this.buildForm();
    this.loadInventory();
this.loading = true;
  
  // Call API
  // Fallback timeout to prevent infinite spinner
  setTimeout(() => {
    this.loading = false;
  }, 2000);
}

  buildForm(): void {
    this.inventoryForm = this.fb.group({
      email: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.email]],
      name: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(2)]],
      quantity: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.min(0)]],
      batchId: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(2)]],
      expireDate: ['', Validators.required],
      price: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.min(0)]],
      category: ['General'],
      supplier: ['Unknown'],
      imagePath: ['']
    });
  }

  get f() {
    return this.inventoryForm.controls;
  }

  searchInventory(): void {
    const keyword = this.searchText.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!keyword) {
      this.filteredItems = [...this.inventoryItems];
      this.updatePagination();
      return;
    }

    this.filteredItems = this.inventoryItems.filter((item) => {
      const name = item.name?.toLowerCase() ?? '';
      const batchId = item.batchId?.toLowerCase() ?? '';
      const category = item.category?.toLowerCase() ?? '';
      const supplier = item.supplier?.toLowerCase() ?? '';

      return name.includes(keyword) || batchId.includes(keyword) || category.includes(keyword) || supplier.includes(keyword);
    });

    this.updatePagination();
  }

  addItem(): void {
    this.isEditing = false;
    this.selectedItem = null;
    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.successMessage = '';
    this.inventoryForm.reset({
      email: '',
      name: '',
      quantity: '',
      batchId: '',
      expireDate: '',
      price: '',
      category: 'General',
      supplier: 'Unknown',
      imagePath: ''
    });
  }

  editItem(item: InventoryModel): void {
    this.isEditing = true;
    this.selectedItem = item;
    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.successMessage = '';
    this.inventoryForm.patchValue({
      email: item.email ?? '',
      name: item.name ?? '',
      quantity: item.quantity ?? '',
      batchId: item.batchId ?? '',
      expireDate: item.expireDate ?? '',
      price: item.price ?? '',
      category: item.category ?? 'General',
      supplier: item.supplier ?? 'Unknown',
      imagePath: item.imagePath ?? ''
    });
  }

  submitInventory(): void {
    if (this.inventoryForm.invalid) {
      this.inventoryForm.markAllAsTouched();
      this.errorMessage = 'Please correct the highlighted fields before saving.';
      return;
    }

    this.loading = true;
    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.successMessage = '';

    const payload = this.inventoryForm.value;
    const formData = new FormData();
    Object.entries(payload).forEach(([key, value]) => {
      if (value !== null && value !== undefined) {
        formData.append(key, String(value));
      }
    });

    const request = this.isEditing && this.selectedItem?._id
      ? this.api.put<{ message: string }>(`/inventory/${this.selectedItem._id}`, Object.fromEntries(Array.from(formData.entries())))
      : this.api.post<{ message: string; inventory?: InventoryModel }>(`/inventory`, Object.fromEntries(Array.from(formData.entries())));

    request.subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.loading = false;
        this.successMessage = this.isEditing ? 'Medicine updated successfully.' : 'Medicine added successfully.';
        this.loadInventory();
        this.addItem();
      },
      error: () => {
        this.loading = false;
        this.errorMessage = this.isEditing ? 'Unable to update medicine. Please try again.' : 'Unable to add medicine. Please try again.';
      }
    });
  }

  deleteItem(item: InventoryModel): void {
    const confirmed = confirm(`Delete "${item.name}" ?`);
    if (!confirmed || !item._id) {
      return;
    }

    this.loading = true;
    this.api.delete<{ message: string }>(`/inventory/${item._id}`).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.loading = false;
        this.successMessage = 'Medicine deleted successfully.';
        this.loadInventory();
      },
      error: () => {
        this.loading = false;
        this.errorMessage = 'Unable to delete medicine. Please try again.';
      }
    });
  }

  refreshTable(): void {
    this.loadInventory();
  }

  getStatusClass(status?: string): string {
    switch (status) {
      case 'In Stock':
        return 'status-success';
      case 'Low Stock':
        return 'status-warning';
      case 'Out Of Stock':
        return 'status-danger';
      default:
        return '';
    }
  }

  trackByItem(index: number, item: InventoryModel): string {
    return item._id ?? `${item.name}-${index}`;
  }

  updatePagination(): void {
    this.totalItems = this.filteredItems.length;
    this.totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(this.totalItems / this.pageSize));

    if (this.currentPage > this.totalPages) {
      this.currentPage = this.totalPages;
    }
  }

  nextPage(): void {
    if (this.currentPage < this.totalPages) {
      this.currentPage++;
    }
  }

  previousPage(): void {
    if (this.currentPage > 1) {
      this.currentPage--;
    }
  }

  sort(column: keyof InventoryModel): void {
    if (this.sortColumn === column) {
      this.sortAscending = !this.sortAscending;
    } else {
      this.sortColumn = column;
      this.sortAscending = true;
    }

    this.filteredItems.sort((a, b) => {
      const valueA = a[column] ?? '';
      const valueB = b[column] ?? '';
      const left = String(valueA).toLowerCase();
      const right = String(valueB).toLowerCase();

      if (left < right) {
        return this.sortAscending ? -1 : 1;
      }

      if (left > right) {
        return this.sortAscending ? 1 : -1;
      }

      return 0;
    });
  }

  resetSearch(): void {
    this.searchText = '';
    this.filteredItems = [...this.inventoryItems];
    this.currentPage = 1;
    this.updatePagination();
  }

  loadInventory(): void {
    this.loading = true;
    this.api.get<InventoryModel[]>('/inventory').subscribe({
      next: (response) => {
        this.inventoryItems = (response ?? []).map((item) => ({
          ...item,
          category: item.category ?? 'General',
          supplier: item.supplier ?? 'Unknown',
          status: this.getDerivedStatus(item.quantity)
        }));
        this.inventoryItemsSignal.set(this.inventoryItems);
        this.filteredItems = [...this.inventoryItems];
        this.updatePagination();
        this.loading = false;
      },
      error: () => {
        this.loading = false;
        this.errorMessage = 'Unable to load medicine stock right now.';
      }
    });
  }

  private getDerivedStatus(quantity: number | string | undefined): 'In Stock' | 'Low Stock' | 'Out Of Stock' {
    const value = Number(quantity);
    if (Number.isNaN(value)) {
      return 'In Stock';
    }
    if (value <= 0) {
      return 'Out Of Stock';
    }
    if (value <= 10) {
      return 'Low Stock';
    }
    return 'In Stock';
  }
}

<div class="page-container">
  <div class="header-header">
    <div>
      <h2 class="page-title">Doctor Orders</h2>
      <p class="page-subtitle">Review new, verified, and picked-up pharmacy orders.</p>
    </div>
    <button type="button" class="btn-refresh" (click)="loadOrders()">Refresh</button>
  </div>

  <div class="tabs">
    <button type="button" (click)="changeTab('new')" [class.active]="activeTab === 'new'">New</button>
    <button type="button" (click)="changeTab('verified')" [class.active]="activeTab === 'verified'">Verified</button>
    <button type="button" (click)="changeTab('picked')" [class.active]="activeTab === 'picked'">Picked Up</button>
  </div>

  <div class="content-card">
    <div class="card-header">
      <h3>{{ getTabTitle() }}</h3>
      <div class="search-box">
        <input
          type="text"
          placeholder="Search by doctor name or email"
          [value]="searchText"
          (input)="searchText = $any($event.target).value; searchOrders()"
        />
      </div>
    </div>

    @if (message) {
      <div class="message" [class.success]="messageType === 'success'" [class.error]="messageType === 'error'">{{ message }}</div>
    }

    <div class="orders-container">
      @if (loading) {
        <div class="loading-state">Loading orders...</div>
      } @else {
        @for (order of filteredOrders; track trackByOrder($index, order)) {
          <div class="order-row">
            <div class="order-meta">
              <strong>{{ order.doctorName }}</strong>
              <span>{{ order.doctorId }}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="order-details">
              <span><strong>Email:</strong> {{ order.email }}</span>
              <span><strong>Medicine:</strong> {{ order.drugNames }}</span>
              <span><strong>Qty:</strong> {{ order.quantity }}</span>
              <span><strong>Total:</strong> â‚¹ {{ order.total }}</span>
              <span><strong>Status:</strong> {{ order.status }}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="row-actions">
              <button type="button" (click)="viewOrder(order)">View</button>
              @if (activeTab === 'new') {
                <button type="button" (click)="verifyOrder(order)">Verify</button>
              }
              @if (activeTab === 'verified') {
                <button type="button" (click)="pickedUpOrder(order)">Picked Up</button>
              }
            </div>
          </div>
        }
      }

      @if (!loading && filteredOrders.length === 0) {
        <div class="empty-state">No orders found for this tab.</div>
      }
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';

interface DoctorOrder {

  id: number;

  doctorName: string;

  doctorContact: string;

  doctorId: string;

  email: string;

  drugNames: string;

  prices: number;

  quantity: number;

  total: number;

  orderDate: string;

  status: 'New' | 'Verified' | 'Picked Up';

}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-orders',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [
    CommonModule,
    FormsModule
  ],
  templateUrl: './orders.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./orders.component.css']
})
export class OrdersComponent implements OnInit {

  /* ==========================================
      PAGE VARIABLES
  ========================================== */

  activeTab: 'new' | 'verified' | 'picked' = 'new';

  loading = false;

  selectedOrder: DoctorOrder | null = null;
  message = '';
  messageType: 'success' | 'error' | '' = '';

  /* ==========================================
      DATA
  ========================================== */

  orders: DoctorOrder[] = [

    {
      id: 1,
      doctorName: 'Lalana Thanthirgama',
      doctorContact: '0716189361',
      doctorId: '9182739182V',
      email: 'doctor@mail.com',
      drugNames: 'Panadol',
      prices: 1200,
      quantity: 3,
      total: 3600,
      orderDate: '22 Aug 2020',
      status: 'New'
    },

    {
      id: 2,
      doctorName: 'John Perera',
      doctorContact: '0774569871',
      doctorId: '92837465V',
      email: 'john@mail.com',
      drugNames: 'Amoxillin',
      prices: 850,
      quantity: 5,
      total: 4250,
      orderDate: '23 Aug 2020',
      status: 'Verified'
    },

    {
      id: 3,
      doctorName: 'David Silva',
      doctorContact: '0715559988',
      doctorId: '83472873V',
      email: 'david@mail.com',
      drugNames: 'Metformin',
      prices: 950,
      quantity: 2,
      total: 1900,
      orderDate: '24 Aug 2020',
      status: 'Picked Up'
    }

  ];

  filteredOrders: DoctorOrder[] = [];

  constructor() {}

  ngOnInit(): void {

    this.filterOrders();

  }
    /* ==========================================
      FILTER ORDERS
  ========================================== */

  filterOrders(): void {

    switch (this.activeTab) {

      case 'new':

        this.filteredOrders = this.orders.filter(

          order => order.status === 'New'

        );

        break;

      case 'verified':

        this.filteredOrders = this.orders.filter(

          order => order.status === 'Verified'

        );

        break;

      case 'picked':

        this.filteredOrders = this.orders.filter(

          order => order.status === 'Picked Up'

        );

        break;

    }

  }

  /* ==========================================
      CHANGE TAB
  ========================================== */

  changeTab(

    tab: 'new' | 'verified' | 'picked'

  ): void {

    this.activeTab = tab;

    this.filterOrders();

  }

  /* ==========================================
      TAB TITLE
  ========================================== */

  getTabTitle(): string {

    switch (this.activeTab) {

      case 'new':

        return 'New Doctor Orders';

      case 'verified':

        return 'Verified Doctor Orders';

      case 'picked':

        return 'Picked Up Orders';

      default:

        return '';

    }

  }

  /* ==========================================
      VERIFY ORDER
  ========================================== */

  verifyOrder(order: DoctorOrder): void {
    order.status = 'Verified';
    this.filterOrders();
    this.showMessage('Order verified successfully.', 'success');
  }

  /* ==========================================
      PICKED UP
  ========================================== */

  pickedUpOrder(order: DoctorOrder): void {
    order.status = 'Picked Up';
    this.filterOrders();
    this.showMessage('Order marked as picked up.', 'success');
  }

  /* ==========================================
      VIEW ORDER
  ========================================== */

  viewOrder(order: DoctorOrder): void {
    this.selectedOrder = order;
    this.showMessage(`Viewing order for ${order.doctorName}.`, 'success');
  }
    /* ==========================================
      SEARCH
  ========================================== */

  searchText = '';

  searchOrders(): void {

    const keyword = this.searchText
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();

    this.filterOrders();

    if (!keyword) {

      return;

    }

    this.filteredOrders = this.filteredOrders.filter(order =>

      order.doctorName.toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||

      order.doctorId.toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||

      order.email.toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||

      order.drugNames.toLowerCase().includes(keyword)

    );

  }

  /* ==========================================
      REFRESH
  ========================================== */

  refreshOrders(): void {

    this.searchText = '';

    this.filterOrders();

  }

  /* ==========================================
      TRACK BY
  ========================================== */

  trackByOrder(

    index: number,

    order: DoctorOrder

  ): number {

    return order.id;

  }

  /* ==========================================
      BACKEND METHODS
  ========================================== */

  loadOrders(): void {
    this.loading = true;

    setTimeout(() => {
      this.loading = false;
      this.filterOrders();
      this.showMessage('Orders refreshed from local pharmacy data.', 'success');
    }, 300);
  }

  saveOrder(order: DoctorOrder): void {
    this.orders = [order, ...this.orders.filter((item) => item.id !== order.id)];
    this.filterOrders();
    this.showMessage('Order saved locally.', 'success');
  }

  /* ==========================================
      LIFECYCLE
  ========================================== */

  private showMessage(message: string, type: 'success' | 'error'): void {
    this.message = message;
    this.messageType = type;
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.message = '';
  }

}

@Component({

  selector:'app-out-of-stock',

  standalone:true,

  imports:[
    CommonModule
  ],

  templateUrl:'./out-of-stock.component.html',

  styleUrls:['./out-of-stock.component.css']

})

export class OutOfStockComponent{

  totalOutOfStock=18;

  criticalItems=7;

  supplierRequests=11;

  estimatedValue=58200;

  stockList=[

    {

      supplierEmail:'lalam12@gmail.com',

      drugName:'Amoxillin',

      quantity:0,

      batchId:'547547654N',

      price:1200,

      supplier:'HealthCare Ltd.',

      status:'Out of Stock'

    },

    {

      supplierEmail:'pharma@gmail.com',

      drugName:'Panadol',

      quantity:0,

      batchId:'PD1023',

      price:980,

      supplier:'ABC Pharma',

      status:'Out of Stock'

    },

    {

      supplierEmail:'wellcare@gmail.com',

      drugName:'Metformin',

      quantity:0,

      batchId:'MF2201',

      price:650,

      supplier:'WellCare Medical',

      status:'Critical'

    }

  ];
    // =====================================================
  // FILTER
  // =====================================================

  selectedFilter = 'all';

  changeFilter(filter: string): void {

    this.selectedFilter = filter;

  }

  // =====================================================
  // REQUEST STOCK
  // =====================================================

  requestStock(item: any): void {

    alert(
      `Stock request has been sent to ${item.supplier} for ${item.drugName}.`
    );

  }

  // =====================================================
  // REFRESH DATA
  // =====================================================

  refreshList(): void {

    window.location.reload();

  }

}

import { Component, OnInit, OnDestroy } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { MatDialogModule, MatDialogRef } from '@angular/material/dialog';
import { Subscription } from 'rxjs';
import { InventoryService } from '../../services/inventory.service';
import { Inventory } from '../../services/inventory.model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-xoutofstock-dialog-box',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, MatDialogModule],
  templateUrl: './xoutofstock-dialog-box.component.html',
  styleUrl: './xoutofstock-dialog-box.component.css'
})
export class XoutofstockDialogBoxComponent implements OnInit, OnDestroy {
  outOfStockItems: Inventory[] = [];
  isLoading = false;
  private inventorySub!: Subscription;

  constructor(
    private inventoryService: InventoryService,
    public dialogRef: MatDialogRef<XoutofstockDialogBoxComponent>
  ) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.inventoryService.getInventories();
    this.inventorySub = this.inventoryService
      .getInventoryUpdateListener()
      .subscribe((items: Inventory[]) => {
        this.isLoading = false;
        // Filter items where quantity is 0 or less
        this.outOfStockItems = (items || []).filter(
          item => !item.quantity || +item.quantity <= 0
        );
      });
  }

  onClose(): void {
    this.dialogRef.close();
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    if (this.inventorySub) {
      this.inventorySub.unsubscribe();
    }
  }
}

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { MatSnackBar, MatSnackBarModule } from '@angular/material/snack-bar';
import { SalesService } from '../../../../services/sales.service';
import * as tf from '@tensorflow/tfjs';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-prediction-report',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, RouterModule, MatSnackBarModule],
  templateUrl: './prediction-report.html',
  styleUrl: './prediction-report.css'
})
export class PredictionReportComponent implements OnInit {
  isLoading = false;
  salesData: any[] = [];
  predictedSales: any[] = [];

  constructor(
    private salesService: SalesService,
    private snackBar: MatSnackBar
  ) {}

  async ngOnInit() {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.salesService.getSalesChartInfo2().subscribe(async (results: any) => {
      this.salesData = results.sales || [];
      await this.runSalesPredictionModel();
      this.isLoading = false;
    });
  }

  async runSalesPredictionModel() {
    if (!this.salesData || this.salesData.length === 0) return;

    // Linear Regression with TensorFlow.js
    const xs = tf.tensor1d(this.salesData.map((_, index) => index + 1));
    const ys = tf.tensor1d(this.salesData.map((item: any) => +item.total));

    const model = tf.sequential();
    model.add(tf.layers.dense({ units: 1, inputShape: [1] }));
    model.compile({ loss: 'meanSquaredError', optimizer: 'sgd' });

    await model.fit(xs, ys, { epochs: 100 });

    const nextMonths = [this.salesData.length + 1, this.salesData.length + 2, this.salesData.length + 3];
    const predictionTensor = model.predict(tf.tensor1d(nextMonths)) as tf.Tensor;
    const predictions = await predictionTensor.data();

    this.predictedSales = nextMonths.map((monthIndex, i) => ({
      month: `Month +${i + 1}`,
      predictedValue: Math.max(0, Math.round(predictions[i]))
    }));

    this.snackBar.open('AI Prediction Calculated Successfully', 'Close', { duration: 3000 });
  }
}

=================================================

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { ReactiveFormsModule, FormGroup, FormControl, Validators, AbstractControl } from '@angular/forms';
import { ActivatedRoute, ParamMap } from '@angular/router';
import { MatSnackBar, MatSnackBarModule } from '@angular/material/snack-bar';
import { SupplierService } from '../../../../services/supplier.service';
import { Supplier } from '../../../../services/supplier.model';

// Custom Validator for Gmail Domain Check
function emailDomainValidator(control: AbstractControl) {
  let email = control.value;
  if (email && email.indexOf('@') !== -1) {
    let [_, domain] = email.split('@');
    if (domain !== 'gmail.com') {
      return {
        emailDomain: {
          parsedDomain: domain
        }
      };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-supplier-form',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, ReactiveFormsModule, MatSnackBarModule],
  templateUrl: './supplier-form.html',
  styleUrl: './supplier-form.css'
})
export class SupplierFormComponent implements OnInit {
  enteredSupplierID = '';
  enteredName = '';
  enteredEmail = '';
  enteredContact = '';
  enteredDrugsAvailable = '';
  supplier!: Supplier;
  isLoading = false;

  form!: FormGroup;
  private mode = 'create';
  private supplierId: string | null = null;

  constructor(
    private supplierService: SupplierService,
    public route: ActivatedRoute,
    private snackBar: MatSnackBar
  ) {}

  ngOnInit() {
    this.form = new FormGroup({
      'supplierID': new FormControl(null, {
        validators: [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(1), Validators.pattern('[0-9]+[Vv]')]
      }),
      'name': new FormControl(null, {
        validators: [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(1)]
      }),
      'email': new FormControl(null, {
        validators: [
          Validators.required,
          Validators.email,
          Validators.minLength(1),
          Validators.pattern('[^ @]*@[^ @]*'),
          emailDomainValidator
        ]
      }),
      'contact': new FormControl(null, {
        validators: [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(1), Validators.pattern('^[0-9]{10,12}$')]
      }),
      'drugsAvailable': new FormControl(null, {
        validators: [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(1)]
      })
    });

    this.route.paramMap.subscribe((paramMap: ParamMap) => {
      if (paramMap.has('supplierId')) {
        this.mode = 'edit';
        this.supplierId = paramMap.get('supplierId');
        this.isLoading = true;
        if (this.supplierId) {
          this.supplierService.getSuppliers(this.supplierId).subscribe((supplierData: any) => {
            this.isLoading = false;
            this.supplier = {
              id: supplierData._id,
              supplierID: supplierData.supplierID || supplierData.supplierId || '',
              name: supplierData.name || '',
              email: supplierData.email || '',
              contact: supplierData.contact || '',
              drugsAvailable: supplierData.drugsAvailable || ''
            };
            this.form.setValue({
              'supplierID': this.supplier.supplierID,
              'name': this.supplier.name,
              'email': this.supplier.email,
              'contact': this.supplier.contact,
              'drugsAvailable': this.supplier.drugsAvailable
            });
          });
        }
      } else {
        this.mode = 'create';
        this.supplierId = null;
      }
    });
  }

  get registerFormControl() {
    return this.form.controls;
  }

  onAddSupplier() {
    if (this.form.invalid) {
      return;
    }

    if (this.mode === 'create') {
      this.supplierService.addSupplier(
        this.form.value.supplierID,
        this.form.value.name,
        this.form.value.email,
        this.form.value.contact,
        this.form.value.drugsAvailable
      );
      this.snackBar.open('Supplier Added Successfully', 'Close', { duration: 3000 });
    } else {
      if (this.supplierId) {
        this.supplierService.updateSupplier(
          this.supplierId,
          this.form.value.supplierID,
          this.form.value.name,
          this.form.value.email,
          this.form.value.contact,
          this.form.value.drugsAvailable
        );
        this.snackBar.open('Supplier Edited!! ', 'Close', { duration: 3000 });
      }
    }

    this.form.reset();
  }
}

=================================================

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { SupplierService } from '../../../../services/supplier.service';
import { Supplier } from '../../../../services/supplier.model';
import { SupplierSearchComponent } from '../supplier-search/supplier-search';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-supplier-inventory',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, SupplierSearchComponent],
  templateUrl: './supplier-inventory.html',
  styleUrl: './supplier-inventory.css'
})
export class SupplierInventoryComponent implements OnInit {
  suppliers: Supplier[] = [];
  filteredSuppliers: Supplier[] = [];
  searchTerm: string = '';
  isLoading = false;

  constructor(private supplierService: SupplierService) {}

  ngOnInit() {
    this.loadSuppliers();
  }

  loadSuppliers() {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.supplierService.getSuppliers().subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.suppliers = data || [];
        this.filteredSuppliers = [...this.suppliers];
        this.isLoading = false;
      },
      error: () => {
        this.isLoading = false;
      }
    });
  }

  onSearch(event?: any) {
    const lower = typeof event === 'string' ? event.toLowerCase() : (this.searchTerm || '').toLowerCase();
    this.filteredSuppliers = this.suppliers.filter(s =>
      (s.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(lower) ||
      (s.company || '').toLowerCase().includes(lower) ||
      (s.supplierId || s.supplierID || '').toLowerCase().includes(lower)
    );
  }

  onDelete(id?: string) {
    if (id) {
      this.supplierService.deleteSupplier(id).subscribe(() => {
        this.loadSuppliers();
      });
    }
  }
}

<div class="supplier-search-bar" style="margin-bottom: 20px;">
  <div class="field">
    <p class="control has-icons-left">
      <input 
        class="input" 
        type="text" 
        placeholder="Search supplier by name, company, or email..." 
        [(ngModel)]="searchTerm" 
        (input)="onSearch()">
      <span class="icon is-small is-left">
        <i class="fa fa-search"></i>
      </span>
    </p>
  </div>
</div>

import { Component, EventEmitter, Output } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-supplier-search',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule],
  templateUrl: './supplier-search.html',
  styleUrl: './supplier-search.css'
})
export class SupplierSearchComponent {
  searchTerm: string = '';

  @Output() searchChanged = new EventEmitter<string>();

  onSearch(): void {
    this.searchChanged.emit(this.searchTerm);
  }
}

FILE: D:\AngularMigration\Angular20\MediCore-Pharmacy-System\MediCore-Pharmacy-System\src\app\pages\suppliers\suppliers.component.ts
=================================================

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule, ReactiveFormsModule, FormGroup, FormControl, Validators } from '@angular/forms';
import { SupplierService } from '../../services/supplier.service';
import { Supplier } from '../../services/supplier.model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-suppliers',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, ReactiveFormsModule],
  templateUrl: './suppliers.component.html',
  styleUrl: './suppliers.component.css'
})
export class SuppliersComponent implements OnInit {
  suppliers: Supplier[] = [];
  filteredSuppliers: Supplier[] = [];
  searchTerm: string = '';
  searchText: string = '';
  isLoading = false;
  loading = false;
  isEditing = false;
  selectedSupplier: Supplier | null = null;
  errorMessage: string = '';
  successMessage: string = '';

  supplierForm!: FormGroup;

  formData: Supplier = {
    name: '',
    email: '',
    contact: '',
    supplierID: '',
    drugsAvailable: ''
  };

  constructor(private supplierService: SupplierService) {}

  ngOnInit() {
    this.initForm();
    this.fetchSuppliers();
  }

  initForm() {
    this.supplierForm = new FormGroup({
      supplierId: new FormControl('', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(3)]),
      name: new FormControl('', [Validators.required]),
      email: new FormControl('', [Validators.required, Validators.email]),
      contact: new FormControl('', [Validators.required, Validators.pattern('^[0-9]{10,12}$')]),
      address: new FormControl('', [Validators.required]),
      company: new FormControl('', [Validators.required]),
      drugs: new FormControl('', [Validators.required])
    });
  }

  get f() {
    return this.supplierForm.controls;
  }

  fetchSuppliers() {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.loading = true;
    this.supplierService.getSuppliers().subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.suppliers = data || [];
        this.filteredSuppliers = [...this.suppliers];
        this.isLoading = false;
        this.loading = false;
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.errorMessage = 'Failed to load suppliers.';
        this.isLoading = false;
        this.loading = false;
      }
    });
  }

  addSupplier() {
    this.resetForm();
  }

  searchSupplier() {
    const value = (this.searchText || this.searchTerm || '').toLowerCase();
    this.filteredSuppliers = this.suppliers.filter(item => {
      return (
        (item.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(value) ||
        (item.supplierId || item.supplierID || '').toLowerCase().includes(value) ||
        (item.company || '').toLowerCase().includes(value) ||
        (item.drugs || item.drugsAvailable || '').toLowerCase().includes(value)
      );
    });
  }

  onSearch() {
    this.searchSupplier();
  }

  refresh() {
    this.searchText = '';
    this.searchTerm = '';
    this.fetchSuppliers();
  }

  submitSupplier() {
    if (this.supplierForm.invalid) {
      this.supplierForm.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }
    const payload = this.supplierForm.value;
    this.loading = true;

    if (this.isEditing && this.selectedSupplier?._id) {
      this.supplierService.updateSupplier(this.selectedSupplier._id, payload).subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.successMessage = 'Supplier updated successfully!';
          this.fetchSuppliers();
          this.resetForm();
        },
        error: () => {
          this.errorMessage = 'Failed to update supplier.';
          this.loading = false;
        }
      });
    } else {
      this.supplierService.createSupplier(payload).subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.successMessage = 'Supplier added successfully!';
          this.fetchSuppliers();
          this.resetForm();
        },
        error: () => {
          this.errorMessage = 'Failed to create supplier.';
          this.loading = false;
        }
      });
    }
  }

  editSupplier(supplier: Supplier) {
    this.isEditing = true;
    this.selectedSupplier = supplier;
    this.supplierForm.patchValue({
      supplierId: supplier.supplierId || supplier.supplierID,
      name: supplier.name,
      email: supplier.email,
      contact: supplier.contact,
      address: supplier.address || '',
      company: supplier.company || supplier.name,
      drugs: supplier.drugs || supplier.drugsAvailable || ''
    });
  }

  onEdit(supplier: Supplier) {
    this.editSupplier(supplier);
  }

  deleteSupplier(supplier: Supplier) {
    if (supplier?._id) {
      this.loading = true;
      this.supplierService.deleteSupplier(supplier._id).subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.successMessage = 'Supplier deleted successfully!';
          this.fetchSuppliers();
        },
        error: () => {
          this.errorMessage = 'Failed to delete supplier.';
          this.loading = false;
        }
      });
    }
  }

  onDelete(supplier: Supplier) {
    this.deleteSupplier(supplier);
  }

  resetForm() {
    this.isEditing = false;
    this.selectedSupplier = null;
    this.supplierForm.reset();
    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.successMessage = '';
  }
}

import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpErrorResponse, HttpHeaders } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable, catchError, throwError } from 'rxjs';

@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class ApiService {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);

  private readonly baseUrl = 'http://localhost:5000/api';
  private readonly defaultHeaders = new HttpHeaders({
    'Content-Type': 'application/json'
  });

  get<T>(path: string, options?: { headers?: HttpHeaders; params?: Record<string, unknown> }): Observable<T> {
    return this.http.get<T>(`${this.baseUrl}${path}`, {
      headers: options?.headers ?? this.defaultHeaders,
      params: options?.params as Record<string, string | number | boolean> | undefined
    }).pipe(catchError(this.handleError));
  }

  post<T>(path: string, body: unknown, options?: { headers?: HttpHeaders }): Observable<T> {
    return this.http.post<T>(`${this.baseUrl}${path}`, body, {
      headers: options?.headers ?? this.defaultHeaders
    }).pipe(catchError(this.handleError));
  }

  put<T>(path: string, body: unknown, options?: { headers?: HttpHeaders }): Observable<T> {
    return this.http.put<T>(`${this.baseUrl}${path}`, body, {
      headers: options?.headers ?? this.defaultHeaders
    }).pipe(catchError(this.handleError));
  }

  delete<T>(path: string, options?: { headers?: HttpHeaders }): Observable<T> {
    return this.http.delete<T>(`${this.baseUrl}${path}`, {
      headers: options?.headers ?? this.defaultHeaders
    }).pipe(catchError(this.handleError));
  }

  private handleError(error: HttpErrorResponse) {
    const message = error.error?.message || error.message || 'Request failed';
    console.error('API Error:', message);
    return throwError(() => new Error(message));
  }
}

import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class EmailService {
  private apiUrl = 'http://localhost:5000/api/email';

  constructor(private http: HttpClient) {}

  sendDoctorOrderEmail(doctorEmail: string, orderDetails: any): Observable<any> {
    const payload = {
      to: doctorEmail,
      subject: 'Doctor Order Status Notification',
      message: `Your prescription order (ID: ${orderDetails.id || orderDetails._id}) status has been updated.`,
      orderDetails
    };
    return this.http.post(`${this.apiUrl}/send`, payload);
  }

  sendSupplierReorderEmail(supplierEmail: string, drugName: string): Observable<any> {
    const payload = {
      to: supplierEmail,
      subject: 'Urgent Drug Re-order Alert',
      message: `The stock for drug "${drugName}" is empty or low. Please dispatch new batch.`
    };
    return this.http.post(`${this.apiUrl}/reorder`, payload);
  }
}

FILE: D:\AngularMigration\Angular20\MediCore-Pharmacy-System\MediCore-Pharmacy-System\src\app\services\inventory.service.ts

import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Subject, Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { map } from 'rxjs/operators';
import { Inventory } from './inventory.model';

export type { Inventory } from './inventory.model';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class InventoryService {
  private inventory: Inventory[] = [];
  private inventoryUpdated = new Subject<Inventory[]>();

  constructor(private http: HttpClient) {}

  getInventory(p0?: number | string, p1?: number): Observable<any> | void {
    if (typeof p0 === 'string') {
      return this.http.get<any>('http://localhost:5000/api/inventory/' + p0);
    }
    const itemsPerPage = typeof p0 === 'number' ? p0 : 0;
    const currentPage = p1 || 0;
    const queryParams = `?pagesize=${itemsPerPage}&page=${currentPage}`;

    this.http.get<{ message: string; inventorys: any }>('http://localhost:5000/api/inventory' + queryParams)
      .pipe(map(inventoryData => {
        return (inventoryData.inventorys || []).map((inv: any) => ({
          email: inv.email,
          name: inv.name,
          quantity: inv.quantity,
          batchId: inv.batchId,
          expireDate: inv.expireDate,
          price: inv.price,
          id: inv._id,
          imagePath: inv.imagePath
        }));
      }))
      .subscribe(transformedInventory => {
        this.inventory = transformedInventory;
        this.inventoryUpdated.next([...this.inventory]);
      });
  }

  getInventories() {
    this.getInventory(0, 0);
  }

  getInventoryUpdateListener() {
    return this.inventoryUpdated.asObservable();
  }

  getInventoryListUpdateListener() {
    return this.getInventoryUpdateListener();
  }

  getInventoryById(id: string): Observable<any> {
    return this.http.get<any>('http://localhost:5000/api/inventory/' + id);
  }

  addInventory(email: string, name: string, quantity: string, batchId: string, expireDate: string, price: string, image: File) {
    const inventoryData = new FormData();
    inventoryData.append('email', email);
    inventoryData.append('name', name);
    inventoryData.append('quantity', quantity);
    inventoryData.append('batchId', batchId);
    inventoryData.append('expireDate', expireDate);
    inventoryData.append('price', price);
    inventoryData.append('image', image, name);

    this.http.post<{ message: string; inventory: Inventory }>('http://localhost:5000/api/inventory', inventoryData)
      .subscribe(() => {
        this.getInventory(0, 0);
      });
  }

  updateInventory(id: string, email: string, name: string, quantity: string, batchId: string, expireDate: string, price: string, image: File | string) {
    let inventoryData: any;
    if (typeof image === 'object') {
      inventoryData = new FormData();
      inventoryData.append('id', id);
      inventoryData.append('email', email);
      inventoryData.append('name', name);
      inventoryData.append('quantity', quantity);
      inventoryData.append('batchId', batchId);
      inventoryData.append('expireDate', expireDate);
      inventoryData.append('price', price);
      inventoryData.append('image', image, name);
    } else {
      inventoryData = { id, email, name, quantity, batchId, expireDate, price, imagePath: image };
    }
    this.http.put('http://localhost:5000/api/inventory/' + id, inventoryData)
      .subscribe(() => {
        this.getInventory(0, 0);
      });
  }

  deleteInventory(inventoryId: string) {
    this.http.delete('http://localhost:5000/api/inventory/' + inventoryId)
      .subscribe(() => {
        this.inventory = this.inventory.filter(item => item.id !== inventoryId);
        this.inventoryUpdated.next([...this.inventory]);
      });
  }

  getExpiredInventory() {
    this.getInventory(0, 0);
  }

  getInventoryExUpdateListener() {
    return this.getInventoryUpdateListener();
  }
}

import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { Subject, Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { map } from 'rxjs/operators';
import { Router } from '@angular/router';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class SalesService {
  private sales: any[] = [];
  private salesUpdated = new Subject<any[]>();

  private salesChart: any[] = [];
  private salesChartUpdated = new Subject<any[]>();

  constructor(private http: HttpClient, private router: Router) {}

  addSales(drugName: Array<any> = [], totalPrice: number, tax: number, paidAmount: number, balance: number) {
    const sales = {
      id: null as any,
      drugName: drugName,
      totalPrice: totalPrice,
      tax: tax,
      paidAmount: paidAmount,
      balance: balance,
      dateTime: null
    };
    this.http.post<{ message: string; salesId: string }>('http://localhost:5000/api/sales', sales)
      .subscribe(responseData => {
        const id = responseData.salesId;
        sales.id = id;
        this.sales.push(sales);
        this.salesUpdated.next([...this.sales]);
      });
  }

  getSales() {
    this.http.get<{ message: string; sales: any }>('http://localhost:5000/api/sales')
      .pipe(map(salesData => {
        return salesData.sales.map((sales: any) => {
          return {
            drugName: sales.drugName,
            dateTime: sales.dateTime,
            totalPrice: sales.totalPrice,
            tax: sales.tax,
            paidAmount: sales.paidAmount,
            balance: sales.balance,
            id: sales._id
          };
        });
      }))
      .subscribe(transformedSales => {
        this.sales = transformedSales;
        this.salesUpdated.next([...this.sales]);
      });
  }

  getSalesChartInfo2(): Observable<any> {
    return this.http.get<{ message: string; sales: any }>('http://localhost:5000/api/sales/getSalesChartInfo');
  }

  getSalesChartInfo() {
    this.http.get<{ message: string; sales: any }>('http://localhost:5000/api/sales/getSalesChartInfo')
      .pipe(map(salesData => {
        return salesData.sales.map((sales: any) => {
          return {
            totalPrice: sales.total,
            dateTime: sales._id,
            drugName: 'null',
            tax: 'null',
            paidAmount: 'null',
            balance: 'null',
            id: 'null'
          };
        });
      }))
      .subscribe(transformedSales => {
        this.salesChart = transformedSales;
        this.salesChartUpdated.next([...this.salesChart]);
      });
  }

  getSalesChartUpdateListener() {
    return this.salesChartUpdated.asObservable();
  }

  getSalesUpdateListener() {
    return this.salesUpdated.asObservable();
  }
}

FILE: D:\AngularMigration\Angular20\MediCore-Pharmacy-System\MediCore-Pharmacy-System\src\app\services\supplier.service.ts

import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable, Subject } from 'rxjs';
import { map } from 'rxjs/operators';
import { Supplier } from './supplier.model';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class SupplierService {
  private suppliers: Supplier[] = [];
  private suppliersUpdated = new Subject<Supplier[]>();

  constructor(private http: HttpClient) {}

  getSuppliers(supplierId?: string): Observable<any> {
    if (supplierId) {
      return this.http.get<any>('http://localhost:5000/api/supplier/' + supplierId);
    }
    return this.http.get<{ message: string; suppliers: any }>('http://localhost:5000/api/supplier')
      .pipe(map(supplierData => {
        return (supplierData.suppliers || []).map((s: any) => ({
          id: s._id,
          _id: s._id,
          supplierID: s.supplierID || s.supplierId || '',
          supplierId: s.supplierID || s.supplierId || '',
          name: s.name || '',
          email: s.email || '',
          contact: s.contact || '',
          drugsAvailable: s.drugsAvailable || '',
          company: s.company || s.name || '',
          drugs: s.drugsAvailable || '',
          status: s.status || 'Active'
        }));
      }));
  }

  getSupplierUpdateListener() {
    return this.suppliersUpdated.asObservable();
  }

  addSupplier(supplierID: string, name: string, email: string, contact: string, drugsAvailable: string) {
    const supplierData: Supplier = {
      supplierID,
      supplierId: supplierID,
      name,
      email,
      contact,
      drugsAvailable
    };
    this.http.post<{ message: string; supplierId: string }>('http://localhost:5000/api/supplier', supplierData)
      .subscribe(responseData => {
        supplierData.id = responseData.supplierId;
        supplierData._id = responseData.supplierId;
        this.suppliers.push(supplierData);
        this.suppliersUpdated.next([...this.suppliers]);
      });
  }

  createSupplier(payload: any): Observable<any> {
    return this.http.post('http://localhost:5000/api/supplier', payload);
  }

  updateSupplier(id: string, payload: any, name?: string, email?: string, contact?: string, drugsAvailable?: string) {
    let supplierData: any = typeof payload === 'object' ? payload : { id, supplierID: payload, name, email, contact, drugsAvailable };
    return this.http.put('http://localhost:5000/api/supplier/' + id, supplierData);
  }

  deleteSupplier(supplierId: string) {
    return this.http.delete('http://localhost:5000/api/supplier/' + supplierId);
  }
}

FILE: D:\AngularMigration\Angular20\MediCore-Pharmacy-System\MediCore-Pharmacy-System\src\app\shared\pipes\inventory-filter-pipe.ts
=================================================

import { Pipe, PipeTransform } from '@angular/core';
import { Inventory } from '../../services/inventory.model';

@Pipe({
  name: 'inventoryFilter',
  standalone: true
})
export class InventoryFilterPipe implements PipeTransform {
  transform(items: Inventory[], searchTerm: string): Inventory[] {
    if (!items || !searchTerm) {
      return items;
    }
    const term = searchTerm.toLowerCase();
    return items.filter(item => 
      item.name?.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
      item.batchId?.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
      item.email?.toLowerCase().includes(term)
    );
  }
}

import { Routes } from '@angular/router';
import { authGuard } from './auth/auth-guard';

// Auth Components
import { Login } from './auth/login/login';
import { Signup } from './auth/signup/signup';
import { DoctorLogin } from './doctor/doctor-login/doctor-login';
import { DoctorSignup } from './doctor/doctor-signup/doctor-signup';

// Suppliers
import { SuppliersComponent } from './pages/suppliers/suppliers.component';

// Inventory
import { Inventory } from './pages/inventory/inventory.component';

// Billing / PoS
import { BillingComponent } from './pages/billing/billing';

// Orders / Doctor Orders
import { OrdersComponent } from './pages/orders/orders.component';

// Expiry & Out Of Stock
import { ExpiredComponent } from './pages/expired/expired.component';
import { OutOfStockComponent } from './pages/out-of-stock/out-of-stock.component';

// Settings & Users
import { SettingsComponent } from './pages/settings/settings.component';
import { DoctorUsersComponent } from './pages/doctor-users/doctor-users.component';

export const routes: Routes = [
  // Default redirect
  { path: '', redirectTo: 'login', pathMatch: 'full' },

  // Suppliers Routes
  { path: 'suppliers', component: SuppliersComponent },

  // Inventory Routes
  { path: 'inventory', component: Inventory },

  // Billing / PoS Routes
  { path: 'billing', component: BillingComponent },
  { path: 'pos', component: BillingComponent },

  // Auth Routes
  { path: 'login', component: Login },
  { path: 'signup', component: Signup },
  { path: 'doctorLogin', component: DoctorLogin },
  { path: 'doctorSignup', component: DoctorSignup },

  // Doctor Orders Routes
  { path: 'doctororders', component: OrdersComponent },

  // Expired & Out Of Stock Routes
  { path: 'expoutofstock', component: ExpiredComponent },
  { path: 'outofstock', component: OutOfStockComponent },

  // Settings & Accounts Routes
  { path: 'settings', component: SettingsComponent },
  { path: 'update/:docId', component: DoctorUsersComponent },
  { path: 'updat/:userId', component: DoctorUsersComponent },

  // Fallback Wildcard
  { path: '**', redirectTo: 'login' }
];
